    collections::{BTreeMap, HashMap, HashSet},
    fs,
    io::{Read, Seek, SeekFrom},
    path::{Path, PathBuf},
    process::{Command, Stdio},
    thread,
    time::{Duration, Instant},
};

/// One positioned source text fragment or raster image. IDs are local to this extractor version.
#[derive(Clone, Debug, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct Block {
    pub id: String,
    pub block_id: String,
    pub page: usize,
    pub page_id: String,
    pub kind: String,
    pub text: String,
    pub bbox: [f64; 4],
    pub size: f64,
    pub color: String,
    pub bold: bool,
    pub path: Option<String>,
}
/// A source page with original page number and dimensions in PDF points.
#[derive(Clone, Debug, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct Page {
    pub id: String,
    pub number: usize,
    pub width: f64,
    pub height: f64,
    pub blocks: Vec<Block>,
}
/// Independently routed evidence; ambiguous boundaries remain explicit.
#[derive(Clone, Debug, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct Section {
    pub id: String,
    pub blocks: Vec<Block>,
    pub boundary: Option<String>,
}
/// Immutable extraction record used to validate on-disk evidence before model calls.
#[derive(Clone, Debug, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct Data {
    pub source: String,
    pub sha256: String,
    pub start: usize,
    pub pages: Vec<Page>,
    pub sections: Vec<Section>,
    pub furniture: Vec<Block>,
}
/// Schema-checked routing result. Assigned is the only status with a target.
#[derive(Clone, Debug, Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]
pub struct Decision {
    pub section_id: String,
    pub status: String,
    pub target: Option<String>,
    pub confidence: f64,
    pub reason: String,
}
/// Validated text evidence reference; block_id is the text unit ID, not its parent.
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]
pub struct Citation {
    pub section_id: String,
    pub block_id: String,
    pub page: usize,
    pub quote: String,
}
/// A cited answer or safe local abstention; unchecked model prose is never displayed on abstention.
#[derive(Clone, Debug, Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]
pub struct Answer {
    pub answer: String,
    pub abstain: bool,
    pub citations: Vec<Citation>,
}
/// Run metadata. No credential, endpoint or conversation fields are persisted.
#[derive(Serialize)]
pub struct Manifest<'a> {
    pub format_version: &'static str,
    pub state: &'a str,
    pub targets: &'a [String],
    pub documents: BTreeMap<String, String>,
    pub decisions: &'a [Decision],
    pub data: &'a Data,
}

/// Escape untrusted PDF strings for both HTML text and quoted attributes.
pub fn escape(text: &str) -> String {
    // One output buffer and one pass; unescaped Unicode is copied without changing it.
    let mut escaped = String::with_capacity(text.len());
    for character in text.chars() {
        match character {
            '&' => escaped.push_str("&amp;"),
            '<' => escaped.push_str("&lt;"),
            '>' => escaped.push_str("&gt;"),
            '"' => escaped.push_str("&quot;"),
            '\'' => escaped.push_str("&#39;"),
            _ => escaped.push(character),
        }
    }
    escaped
}

/// Execute a local converter with bounded runtime and no shell interpolation or provider data.
pub fn run_tool(program: &str, args: &[&std::ffi::OsStr]) -> Result<()> {
    run_tool_to(program, args, Stdio::null())
}

/// Redirect converter output to a file when needed; this avoids blocking on a full pipe.
fn run_tool_to(program: &str, args: &[&std::ffi::OsStr], stdout: Stdio) -> Result<()> {
    let mut child = Command::new(program)
        .args(args)
        .stdin(Stdio::null())
        .stdout(stdout)
        .env("LC_ALL", "C")
        .stderr(Stdio::null())
        .spawn()
        .map_err(|_| {
            anyhow::anyhow!(
                "Cannot start {program}; install Poppler and ensure its tools are on PATH."
            )
        })?;
    let start = Instant::now();
    loop {
        if let Some(status) = child
            .try_wait()
            .context("Cannot inspect converter process")?
        {
            ensure!(
                status.success(),
                "{program} failed; check PDF validity, encryption, permissions and Poppler installation."
            );
            return Ok(());
        }
        if start.elapsed() > Duration::from_secs(180) {
            let _ = child.kill();
            let _ = child.wait();
            bail!("{program} exceeded the 180-second conversion timeout.");
        }
        thread::sleep(Duration::from_millis(20));
    }
}

/// Read page count once from Poppler metadata; no source text leaves this machine.
fn page_count(source: &Path) -> Result<usize> {
    let mut output = tempfile::tempfile().context("Cannot create metadata scratch file")?;
    run_tool_to(
        "pdfinfo",
        &[source.as_os_str()],
        Stdio::from(output.try_clone()?),
    )?;
    output.seek(SeekFrom::Start(0))?;
    let mut metadata = String::new();
    output
        .read_to_string(&mut metadata)
        .context("Cannot read PDF metadata")?;
    let count = metadata
        .lines()
        .find_map(|line| line.strip_prefix("Pages:"))
        .context("Missing PDF page count")?
        .trim()
        .parse::<usize>()
        .context("Invalid PDF page count")?;
    ensure!(count > 0, "PDF has no pages.");
    Ok(count)
}

/// Accept literal, quoted, shell-escaped and home-relative single PDF paths.
pub fn pdf_path(input: &str) -> Result<PathBuf> {
    let input = input.trim();
    let expand = |s: &str| -> PathBuf {
        if let Some(tail) = s.strip_prefix("~/")
            && let Some(home) = std::env::var_os("HOME").or_else(|| std::env::var_os("USERPROFILE"))
        {
            return PathBuf::from(home).join(tail);
        }
        PathBuf::from(s)
    };
    let literal = expand(input);
    let path = if literal.is_file() {
        literal
    } else {
        let parts =
            shell_words::split(input).context("Invalid path quoting; type or drop one PDF path")?;
        ensure!(
            parts.len() == 1,
            "Enter one PDF path; quote or escape spaces if needed."
        );
        expand(&parts[0])
    };
    ensure!(
        path.is_file()
            && path
                .extension()
                .is_some_and(|s| s.eq_ignore_ascii_case("pdf")),
        "Choose one existing PDF file."
    );
    path.canonicalize().context("Cannot resolve PDF path")
}

/// Read XML attributes with entity decoding; numeric conversion is checked separately.
fn attributes(
    event: &quick_xml::events::BytesStart<'_>,
    reader: &Reader<&[u8]>,
) -> Result<BTreeMap<String, String>> {
    event
        .attributes()
        .map(|a| {
            let a = a?;
            Ok((
                String::from_utf8(a.key.as_ref().to_vec())?,
                a.decoded_and_normalized_value(
                    quick_xml::XmlVersion::Implicit1_0,
                    reader.decoder(),
                )?
                .into_owned(),
            ))
        })
        .collect()
}
/// Extract a finite XML numeric attribute; never silently replace a bad coordinate.
fn number(attrs: &BTreeMap<String, String>, key: &str) -> Result<f64> {
    let value: f64 = attrs
        .get(key)
        .context("Missing XML coordinate")?
        .parse()
        .context("Invalid XML coordinate")?;
    ensure!(value.is_finite(), "Nonfinite XML coordinate.");
    Ok(value)
}

/// Hash large PDFs with bounded memory while retaining the exact content-derived IDs.
fn file_sha256(source: &Path) -> Result<String> {
    let mut file = fs::File::open(source).context("Cannot read PDF")?;
    let mut digest = Sha256::new();
    let mut buffer = [0_u8; 64 * 1024];
    loop {
        let count = match file.read(&mut buffer) {
            Err(error) if error.kind() == std::io::ErrorKind::Interrupted => continue,
            result => result.context("Cannot read PDF")?,
        };
        if count == 0 {
            break;
        }
        digest.update(&buffer[..count]);
    }
    Ok(format!("{:x}", digest.finalize()))
}

/// Convert through Poppler XML in a disposable directory; retain only our own HTML and images.
pub fn extract(source: &Path, run: &Path, workers: usize) -> Result<Data> {
    fs::create_dir_all(run.join("images")).context("Cannot create output image directory")?;
    let scratch = tempfile::tempdir_in(run).context("Cannot create conversion workspace")?;
    let source = source.canonicalize().context("Cannot read PDF path")?;
    let count = page_count(&source)?;
    ensure!(
        (1..=4).contains(&workers),
        "PDF worker count must be between 1 and 4."
    );
    let workers = workers
        .min(
            thread::available_parallelism()
                .map(usize::from)
                .unwrap_or(1),
        )
        .min(count);
    let source_name = source
        .file_name()
        .context("Missing PDF filename")?
        .to_string_lossy()
        .into_owned();
    let sha256 = file_sha256(&source)?;
    let slice = Regex::new(r"(?i)^(.+)_pages_(\d+)-(\d+)\.pdf$")?;
    let offset = slice.captures(&source_name);
    let start = offset
        .as_ref()
        .map(|c| c[2].parse::<usize>())
        .transpose()?
        .unwrap_or(1);
    ensure!(start > 0, "Original page numbers must be positive.");
    if let Some(c) = offset {
        ensure!(
            c[3].parse::<usize>()?.checked_sub(start).map(|n| n + 1) == Some(count),
            "Page count does not match slice filename."
        );
    }
    let span = count.div_ceil(workers);
    // Only independent conversion ranges run concurrently. Merge all pages before segmentation
    // so a section crossing a worker boundary retains its original ownership and order.
    let mut pages = thread::scope(|scope| -> Result<Vec<Page>> {
        let mut handles = Vec::new();
        for first in (1..=count).step_by(span) {
            let last = (first + span - 1).min(count);
            let source = &source;
            let hash = &sha256;
            let scratch = scratch.path();
            handles.push(scope.spawn(move || -> Result<Vec<Page>> {
                let folder = tempfile::tempdir_in(scratch)?;
                let xml = folder.path().join("evidence.xml");
                run_tool(
                    "pdftohtml",
                    &[
                        "-q".as_ref(),
                        "-xml".as_ref(),
                        "-hidden".as_ref(),
                        "-nomerge".as_ref(),
                        "-noroundcoord".as_ref(),
                        "-zoom".as_ref(),
                        "1".as_ref(),
                        "-fmt".as_ref(),
                        "png".as_ref(),
                        "-f".as_ref(),
                        first.to_string().as_ref(),
                        "-l".as_ref(),
                        last.to_string().as_ref(),
                        source.as_os_str(),
                        xml.as_os_str(),
                    ],
                )?;
                let contents = fs::read_to_string(xml).context("Cannot read converter XML")?;
                let pages = parse_xml(&contents, hash, start, first, folder.path(), run)?;
                ensure!(
                    pages.len() == last - first + 1,
                    "Converter omitted pages in a range."
                );
                Ok(pages)
            }));
        }
        let mut pages = Vec::new();
        for handle in handles {
            pages.extend(
                handle
                    .join()
                    .map_err(|_| anyhow::anyhow!("PDF conversion worker failed"))??,
            );
        }
        Ok(pages)
    })?;
    ensure!(pages.len() == count, "Converter page count mismatch.");
    // Poppler emits images before text; geometric ordering reconnects local image references.
    for page in &mut pages {
        page.blocks.sort_by(|a, b| {
            a.bbox[1]
                .total_cmp(&b.bbox[1])
                .then(a.bbox[0].total_cmp(&b.bbox[0]))
        });
    }
    let (sections, furniture) = segment(&pages)?;
    let data = Data {
        source: source_name,
        sha256,
        start,
        pages,
        sections,
        furniture,
    };
    let mut source_sections = data.sections.clone();
    source_sections.push(Section {
        id: "furniture".into(),
        blocks: data.furniture.clone(),
        boundary: None,
    });
    fs::write(
        run.join("source.html"),
        html_document("Source PDF", &data, &source_sections, run)?,
    )
    .context("Cannot write source HTML")?;
    Ok(data)
}

/// Parse Poppler XML without resolving external DTDs. Normalize extracted PNG/JPEG images to PNG.
fn parse_xml(
    contents: &str,
    hash: &str,
    start: usize,
    first: usize,
    scratch: &Path,
    run: &Path,
) -> Result<Vec<Page>> {
    let mut reader = Reader::from_str(contents);
    reader.config_mut().expand_empty_elements = true;
    let mut pages: Vec<Page> = Vec::new();
    let mut fonts: HashMap<String, (f64, String)> = HashMap::new();
    let mut active: Option<Block> = None;
    // Each worker has one private conversion folder. Resolve its root once on first image;
    // still resolve every image path individually so symlink escapes remain rejected.
    let mut scratch_root: Option<PathBuf> = None;
    loop {
        match reader.read_event().context("Invalid converter XML")? {
            Event::Start(event) => {
                let attrs = attributes(&event, &reader)?;
                match event.name().as_ref() {
                    b"page" => {
                        let local: usize = attrs
                            .get("number")
                            .context("Missing page number")?
                            .parse()?;
                        ensure!(
                            local == first + pages.len(),
                            "Unexpected or duplicate converter page order."
                        );
                        let n = start + local - 1;
                        pages.push(Page {
                            id: format!("r{}-p{n:04}", &hash[..12]),
                            number: n,
                            width: number(&attrs, "width")?,
                            height: number(&attrs, "height")?,
                            blocks: vec![],
                        });
                    }
                    b"fontspec" => {
                        fonts.insert(
                            attrs.get("id").context("Missing font id")?.clone(),
                            (
                                number(&attrs, "size")?,
                                attrs
                                    .get("color")
                                    .cloned()
                                    .unwrap_or_else(|| "#000000".into()),
                            ),
                        );
                    }
                    b"text" | b"image" => {
                        let page = pages.last_mut().context("Evidence outside a page")?;
                        let id = format!("{}-u{:04}", page.id, page.blocks.len());
                        let x = number(&attrs, "left")?;
                        let y = number(&attrs, "top")?;
                        let w = number(&attrs, "width")?;
                        let h = number(&attrs, "height")?;
                        ensure!(w >= 0.0 && h >= 0.0, "Invalid evidence dimensions.");
                        let (size, color) = attrs
                            .get("font")
                            .and_then(|id| fonts.get(id))
                            .cloned()
                            .unwrap_or((11.0, "#000000".into()));
                        let is_image = event.name().as_ref() == b"image";
                        let mut block = Block {
                            id: id.clone(),
                            block_id: id.clone(),
                            page: page.number,
                            page_id: page.id.clone(),
                            kind: if is_image { "image" } else { "text" }.into(),
                            text: String::new(),
                            bbox: [x, y, x + w, y + h],
                            size,
                            color,
                            bold: false,
                            path: None,
                        };
                        if is_image {
                            let src = PathBuf::from(
                                attrs.get("src").context("Missing local image source")?,
                            );
                            let src = if src.is_absolute() {
                                src
                            } else {
                                scratch.join(src)
                            };
                            let src = src.canonicalize().context("Missing converted image")?;
                            if scratch_root.is_none() {
                                scratch_root = Some(scratch.canonicalize()?);
                            }
                            ensure!(
                                src.starts_with(scratch_root.as_ref().expect("resolved scratch root")),
                                "Converted image path escaped the workspace."
                            );
                            let relative = format!("images/{id}.png");
                            // Poppler already emits PNG here for most PDFs. Preserve those bytes;
                            // decoding and recompressing every image dominates large-document runtime.
                            let image = image::ImageReader::open(&src)
                                .context("Cannot open converted image")?
                                .with_guessed_format()?;
                            if image.format() == Some(image::ImageFormat::Png) {
                                fs::copy(&src, run.join(&relative))
                                    .context("Cannot save PNG image")?;
                            } else {
                                image
                                    .decode()
                                    .context("Cannot decode converted image")?
                                    .save(run.join(&relative))
                                    .context("Cannot normalize PNG image")?;
                            }
                            block.path = Some(relative);
                            page.blocks.push(block);
                        } else {
                            ensure!(active.is_none(), "Unexpected nested text evidence.");
                            active = Some(block);
                        }
                    }
                    b"b" => {
                        if let Some(b) = active.as_mut() {
                            b.bold = true;
                        }
                    }
                    _ => {}
                }
            }
            Event::Text(text) => {
                if let Some(b) = active.as_mut() {
                    b.text.push_str(&text.xml10_content()?);
                }
            }
            Event::GeneralRef(reference) => {
                if let Some(b) = active.as_mut() {
                    if let Some(c) = reference.resolve_char_ref()? {
                        b.text.push(c);
                    } else {
                        let name = reference.decode()?;
                        let entity = match name.as_ref() {
                            "amp" => "&",
                            "lt" => "<",
                            "gt" => ">",
                            "quot" => "\"",
                            "apos" => "'",
                            _ => bail!("Unknown XML text entity."),
                        };
                        b.text.push_str(entity);
                    }
                }
            }
            Event::CData(text) => {
                if let Some(b) = active.as_mut() {
                    b.text.push_str(&text.decode()?);
                }
            }
            Event::End(event) if event.name().as_ref() == b"text" => {
                let b = active.take().context("Unexpected text close")?;
                pages.last_mut().context("No page")?.blocks.push(b);
            }
            Event::Eof => break,
            _ => {}
        }
    }
    ensure!(active.is_none(), "Unclosed XML evidence text.");
    Ok(pages)
}

/// Recognize known evidence templates, otherwise use whole pages as candidates.
pub fn segment(pages: &[Page]) -> Result<(Vec<Section>, Vec<Block>)> {
    let heading = Regex::new(
        r"(?i)^(?:Service (?:start evidence|stop evidence|health snapshot)|Process state verification|Maintenance sequence evidence)\b|^Evidence:",
    )?;
    let furniture = Regex::new(
        r"^(?:Page \d+|Operational Evidence Record|Synthetic Server Operations - .+|synthetic evidence \| page \d+ \| fictional evaluation material|This page contains independent operational evidence for two server/process streams\. The visual format is|intentionally shared so classification depends on semantic evidence and relationships rather than page styling\.)$",
    )?;
    let is_heading = |b: &Block| {
        b.kind == "text"
            && (heading.is_match(b.text.trim())
                || (["Start", "Stop"].contains(&b.text.trim()) && b.size >= 12.0))
    };
    let marked = pages.iter().flat_map(|p| &p.blocks).any(is_heading);
    let mut sections = Vec::new();
    let mut excluded = Vec::new();
    let mut current: Option<Section> = None;
    for page in pages {
        for b in &page.blocks {
            if b.kind == "text" && furniture.is_match(b.text.trim()) {
                excluded.push(b.clone());
                continue;
            }
            if marked && is_heading(b) {
                if let Some(mut old) = current.take() {
                    old.boundary =
                        Some("New heading before an explicit evidence-close marker.".into());
                    sections.push(old);
                }
                current = Some(Section {
                    id: format!("s-{}", b.id),
                    blocks: vec![],
                    boundary: None,
                });
            }
            let section = current.get_or_insert_with(|| Section {
                id: format!("s-{}", b.id),
                blocks: vec![],
                boundary: Some(
                    if marked {
                        "No recognized opening heading."
                    } else {
                        "Unmarked page; assess whether evidence has one identity or is mixed."
                    }
                    .into(),
                ),
            });
            section.blocks.push(b.clone());
            if marked && b.text.to_lowercase().starts_with("evidence section closed") {
                sections.push(current.take().expect("open section"));
            }
        }
        if !marked && let Some(section) = current.take() {
            sections.push(section);
        }
    }
    if let Some(mut section) = current {
        section.boundary = Some("No explicit close marker before end of input.".into());
        sections.push(section);
    }
    Ok((sections, excluded))
}

/// Render original selected units with provenance attributes and self-contained local PNGs.
pub fn html_document(title: &str, data: &Data, sections: &[Section], run: &Path) -> Result<String> {
    let owners: HashMap<_, _> = sections
        .iter()
        .flat_map(|s| s.blocks.iter().map(move |b| (&b.id, &s.id)))
        .collect();
    let mut body = format!(
        "<!doctype html><html><head><meta charset=\"utf-8\"><title>{}</title><style>body{{font-family:sans-serif;background:#eee;padding:20px}}.page{{position:relative;background:white;overflow:hidden;margin-bottom:24px}}</style></head><body><h1>{}</h1><p>Original source coordinates; omitted evidence leaves blank areas.</p>",
        escape(title),
        escape(title)
    );
    for page in &data.pages {
        let blocks: Vec<_> = page
            .blocks
            .iter()
            .filter(|b| owners.contains_key(&b.id))
            .collect();
        if blocks.is_empty() {
            continue;
        }
        body.push_str(&format!(
            "<h2>{} — source page {}</h2><div class=\"page\" style=\"width:{}pt;height:{}pt\">",
            escape(&data.source),
            page.number,
            page.width,
            page.height
        ));
        for b in blocks {
            let attrs = format!(
                "id=\"{}\" data-section=\"{}\" data-block=\"{}\" data-page=\"{}\"",
                escape(&b.id),
                escape(owners[&b.id]),
                escape(&b.block_id),
                b.page
            );
            let [x, y, x1, y1] = b.bbox;
            if b.kind == "image" {
                let bytes = fs::read(run.join(b.path.as_ref().context("Missing image path")?))
                    .context("Missing or unreadable image; reconvert the PDF")?;
                body.push_str(&format!("<img {attrs} alt=\"Evidence image {}\" style=\"position:absolute;left:{x}pt;top:{y}pt;width:{}pt;height:{}pt\" src=\"data:image/png;base64,{}\">",escape(&b.id),x1-x,y1-y,STANDARD.encode(bytes)));
            } else {
                // This fixed ASCII format needs no regex compilation for each document.
                let color = if b.color.len() == 7
                    && b.color.starts_with('#')
                    && b.color.as_bytes()[1..].iter().all(u8::is_ascii_hexdigit)
                {
                    &b.color
                } else {
                    "#000000"
                };
                body.push_str(&format!("<div {attrs}><span style=\"position:absolute;white-space:pre;left:{x}pt;top:{y}pt;font-size:{}pt;color:{color};font-weight:{}\">{}</span></div>",b.size,if b.bold{"bold"}else{"normal"},escape(&b.text)));
            }
        }
        body.push_str("</div>");
    }
    body.push_str("</body></html>");
    Ok(body)
}

/// Validate on-disk wrappers against original provenance, returning only text and image reference IDs.
pub fn read_html(path: &Path, sections: &[Section], furniture: &[Block]) -> Result<Vec<Value>> {
    let content =
        fs::read_to_string(path).context("Cannot read evidence HTML; reconvert the PDF")?;
    let html = Html::parse_document(&content);
    let selector = Selector::parse("[data-section]").expect("static selector");
    let mut expected: HashMap<&str, (&str, &Block)> = sections
        .iter()
        .flat_map(|s| {
            s.blocks
                .iter()
                .map(move |b| (b.id.as_str(), (s.id.as_str(), b)))
        })
        .collect();
    for b in furniture {
        expected.insert(&b.id, ("furniture", b));
    }
    let mut found: HashMap<String, Value> = HashMap::new();
    for node in html.select(&selector) {
        let attrs = node.value();
        let id = attrs.attr("id").unwrap_or("");
        let (owner, b) = expected
            .get(id)
            .context("HTML contains foreign evidence IDs; reconvert")?;
        ensure!(
            !found.contains_key(id),
            "HTML contains duplicate evidence IDs."
        );
        ensure!(
            attrs.attr("data-section") == Some(*owner)
                && attrs.attr("data-block") == Some(b.block_id.as_str())
                && attrs.attr("data-page") == Some(b.page.to_string().as_str())
                && attrs.name() == if b.kind == "image" { "img" } else { "div" },
            "HTML provenance was modified."
        );
        let text = if b.kind == "text" {
            let text = node.text().collect::<String>();
            ensure!(
                text == b.text,
                "HTML evidence text was modified; reconvert."
            );
            text
        } else {
            "[Local image reference; pixels not provided]".into()
        };
        found.insert(
            id.into(),
            json!({"id":id,"parent_block_id":b.block_id,"page":b.page,"text":text}),
        );
    }
    ensure!(
        found.len() == expected.len(),
        "HTML is missing evidence blocks; reconvert."
    );
    Ok(sections.iter().map(|s|json!({"section_id":s.id,"blocks":s.blocks.iter().map(|b|found[&b.id].clone()).collect::<Vec<_>>()})).collect())
}

/// Exact optional user labels; automatic discovery is used when no payload is supplied.
pub fn load_targets(path: &Path) -> Result<Vec<String>> {
    let v: Value =
        serde_json::from_str(&fs::read_to_string(path).context("Cannot read target payload")?)
            .context("Invalid target JSON")?;
    let targets: Vec<String> = serde_json::from_value(v["targets"].clone())
        .context("Use {\"targets\":[\"name-a\",\"name-b\"]}")?;
    ensure!(
        targets.len() >= 2
            && targets
                .iter()
                .all(|s| !s.trim().is_empty() && s.chars().count() <= 200)
            && targets.iter().collect::<HashSet<_>>().len() == targets.len(),
        "Supply at least two distinct nonempty target names, at most 200 characters each."
    );
    Ok(targets)
}

/// Return discovered names in first appearance order.
pub fn discovered(decisions: &[Decision]) -> Vec<String> {
    let mut result = Vec::new();
    let mut seen = HashSet::new();
    for d in decisions {
        if d.status == "assigned"
            && let Some(name) = &d.target
            && seen.insert(name)
        {
            result.push(name.clone());
        }
    }
    result
}

/// Local baseline decisions remain available if any later provider request fails.
pub fn pending(data: &Data) -> Vec<Decision> {
    data.sections
        .iter()
        .map(|s| Decision {
            section_id: s.id.clone(),
            status: "uncertain".into(),
            target: None,
            confidence: 0.0,
            reason: s
                .boundary
                .clone()
                .unwrap_or_else(|| "Not classified; local conversion only.".into()),
        })
        .collect()
}

/// Route one original section per model request; never infer image contents or rewrite source evidence.
pub fn classify(
    data: &Data,
    targets: Option<&[String]>,
    model: &dyn Model,
    run: &Path,
) -> Result<Vec<Decision>> {
    let contexts = read_html(&run.join("source.html"), &data.sections, &data.furniture)?;
    let instruction = if targets.is_some() {
        "Assign evidence to one exact supplied process/service target. Use unmatched if none matches. "
    } else {
        "Discover the primary named process/entity using headings and identifying fields. Infer grouping level from this document, not a fixed preference for hosts, services or operations. Copy the exact identifying name into target; never invent it. Use known_groups consistently only for the same identity. Keep unrelated identities separate even on shared infrastructure. "
    };
    let instruction = format!(
        "{instruction}Start/stop records for the same identity belong together. Similar identifiers stay separate. Images are unseen; nearby text may establish ownership. Assess boundary notes conservatively. Use mixed for inseparable different identities, uncertain for ambiguous/missing identity. Only assigned may have non-null target. Copy section_id exactly. Explain identity using source text. Confidence is an estimate."
    );
    let schema = decision_schema();
    let mut decisions = Vec::new();
    for (i, (section, context)) in data.sections.iter().zip(contexts).enumerate() {
        let text = section
            .blocks
            .iter()
            .filter(|b| b.kind == "text")
            .map(|b| b.text.as_str())
            .collect::<Vec<_>>()
            .join("\n");
        let d = if (targets.is_some() && section.boundary.is_some()) || text.trim().is_empty() {
            Decision {
                section_id: section.id.clone(),
                status: "uncertain".into(),
                target: None,
                confidence: 0.0,
                reason: section.boundary.clone().unwrap_or_else(|| {
                    "Image-only identity is unavailable to this text model.".into()
                }),
            }
        } else {
            let mut payload = json!({"evidence":context,"boundary_note":section.boundary});
            if let Some(names) = targets {
                payload["targets"] = json!(names);
            } else {
                payload["known_groups"] = json!(discovered(&decisions));
            }
            let value = model.complete(&instruction, &payload, &schema)?;
            ensure!(
                validate(&value, &schema),
                "Invalid classification result schema."
            );
            let d: Decision = serde_json::from_value(value)?;
            ensure!(
                d.section_id == section.id
                    && ((d.status == "assigned"
                        && d.target.as_ref().is_some_and(|s| !s.is_empty()))
                        || (d.status != "assigned" && d.target.is_none())),
                "Invalid section ID or target/status pair."
            );
            if let Some(label) = &d.target {
                if let Some(names) = targets {
                    ensure!(
                        names.contains(label),
                        "Assignment uses a target outside the payload."
                    );
                } else {
                    let boundary = Regex::new(&format!(
                        r"(?:^|[^\w.-]){}(?:$|[^\w.-])",
                        regex::escape(label)
                    ))?;
                    ensure!(
                        label.trim() == label
                            && label.chars().count() <= 200
                            && !label.chars().any(char::is_control)
                            && boundary.is_match(&text),
                        "Discovered group name is not an exact identifier in source evidence."
                    );
                }
            }
            d
        };
        decisions.push(d);
        eprintln!("Classified {}/{} sections", i + 1, data.sections.len());
    }
    Ok(decisions)
}

/// Select only assigned evidence for a group, or withheld sections for None.
pub fn selected(data: &Data, decisions: &[Decision], target: Option<&str>) -> Vec<Section> {
    let ids: HashSet<_> = decisions
        .iter()
        .filter(|d| match target {
            Some(t) => d.status == "assigned" && d.target.as_deref() == Some(t),
            None => d.status != "assigned",
        })
        .map(|d| &d.section_id)
        .collect();
    data.sections
        .iter()
        .filter(|s| ids.contains(&s.id))
        .cloned()
        .collect()
}

/// Export only complete decision sets; use a versioned Rust manifest (not Python-compatible IDs).
pub fn export(
    data: &Data,
    targets: &[String],
    decisions: &[Decision],
    run: &Path,
    state: &str,
) -> Result<BTreeMap<String, String>> {
    let ids: HashSet<_> = decisions.iter().map(|d| &d.section_id).collect();
    ensure!(
        ids.len() == decisions.len()
            && ids.len() == data.sections.len()
            && data.sections.iter().all(|s| ids.contains(&s.id)),
        "Export refused: duplicate, missing or foreign decisions."
    );
    let schema = decision_schema();
    for d in decisions {
        ensure!(
            validate(&serde_json::to_value(d)?, &schema)
                && if d.status == "assigned" {
                    d.target.as_ref().is_some_and(|t| targets.contains(t))
                } else {
                    d.target.is_none()
                },
            "Export refused: invalid decision or target/status pair."
        );
    }
    let mut documents = BTreeMap::new();
    let slug = Regex::new("[^a-z0-9]+")?;
    for (i, target) in targets.iter().enumerate() {
        let name = slug
            .replace_all(&target.to_lowercase(), "-")
            .trim_matches('-')
            .chars()
            .take(60)
            .collect::<String>();
        let name = format!(
            "process-{:02}-{}.html",
            i + 1,
            if name.is_empty() { "group" } else { &name }
        );
        fs::write(
            run.join(&name),
            html_document(target, data, &selected(data, decisions, Some(target)), run)?,
        )?;
        documents.insert(target.clone(), name);
    }
    fs::write(
        run.join("withheld.html"),
        html_document(
            "Withheld evidence",
            data,
            &selected(data, decisions, None),
            run,
        )?,
    )?;
    fs::write(
        run.join("furniture.html"),
        html_document(
            "Audited furniture",
            data,
            &[Section {
                id: "furniture".into(),
                blocks: data.furniture.clone(),
                boundary: None,
            }],
            run,
        )?,
    )?;
    let manifest = Manifest {
        format_version: "rust-poppler-v1",
        state,
        targets,
        documents: documents.clone(),
        decisions,
        data,
    };
    fs::write(
        run.join("manifest.json"),
        serde_json::to_string_pretty(&manifest)?,
    )?;
    let mut index = format!(
        "<!doctype html><meta charset=\"utf-8\"><title>Evidence review</title><h1>Evidence review — {}</h1><ul>",
        escape(state)
    );
    for (label, file) in &documents {
        index.push_str(&format!(
            "<li><a href=\"{}\">{}</a></li>",
            escape(file),
            escape(label)
        ));
    }
    for file in [
        "source.html",
        "withheld.html",
        "furniture.html",
        "manifest.json",
    ] {
        index.push_str(&format!("<li><a href=\"{file}\">{file}</a></li>"));
    }
    index.push_str("</ul><p>Confidence is a model estimate, not measured accuracy.</p><table border=\"1\"><tr><th>Section</th><th>Target</th><th>Status</th><th>Confidence</th><th>Reason</th></tr>");
    for d in decisions {
        index.push_str(&format!(
            "<tr><td>{}</td><td>{}</td><td>{}</td><td>{}</td><td>{}</td></tr>",
            escape(&d.section_id),
            escape(d.target.as_deref().unwrap_or("")),
            escape(&d.status),
            d.confidence,
            escape(&d.reason)
        ));
    }
    index.push_str("</table>");
    fs::write(run.join("index.html"), index)?;
    Ok(documents)
}

/// Resolve only a selected process's image; reject foreign IDs and symlink/path escapes.
pub fn image_for_process(
    data: &Data,
    decisions: &[Decision],
    target: Option<&str>,
    id: &str,
    run: &Path,
) -> Result<PathBuf> {
    for section in selected(data, decisions, target) {
        for b in section.blocks {
            if b.id == id && b.kind == "image" {
                let path = run
                    .join(b.path.context("Missing image path")?)
                    .canonicalize()
                    .context("Selected image is missing; reconvert")?;
                ensure!(
                    path.starts_with(run.join("images").canonicalize()?) && path.is_file(),
                    "Image path is outside this run."
                );
                return Ok(path);
            }
        }
    }
    bail!("Image ID is not part of the selected process.")
}

/// Shared generic chat instruction: partial supported answers are preferable to blanket abstention.
pub const CHAT_INSTRUCTION: &str = "Answer using ONLY supplied selected-process evidence. For multipart questions, answer each supported part and explicitly state which parts are not established by the supplied text; do not abstain entirely just because one part is missing. Abstain only when no useful part can be supported or a relevant conflict cannot be resolved. Start and stop at different times are not inherently contradictory. Distinguish services/entities within a group; a hostname is not necessarily one unique service. Cite every factual claim with section_id, block_id copied from a text block id, original page, and an exact nonempty quote from that single text block. parent_block_id is NOT a citation ID. Never cite images or infer their pixels. Non-abstaining answers require citations. A batch or chunk covers only supplied evidence; never claim process-wide absence or completeness.";

/// Isolated in-memory conversations; selected HTML is re-read for every question.
#[derive(Default)]
pub struct Chat {
    pub histories: HashMap<(String, Option<usize>), Vec<Value>>,
    pub notice: String,
}

/// Safe locally generated abstention, never unchecked provider prose.
fn abstain(message: &str) -> Answer {
    Answer {
        answer: message.into(),
        abstain: true,
        citations: vec![],
    }
}

/// Exact citation validation against only the batch actually sent to the model.
fn valid_citations(answer: &mut Answer, batch: &[Value]) -> bool {
    let mut valid = answer.abstain || !answer.citations.is_empty();
    for citation in &mut answer.citations {
        let blocks: Vec<_> = batch
            .iter()
            .filter(|s| s["section_id"] == citation.section_id)
            .flat_map(|s| s["blocks"].as_array().into_iter().flatten())
            .filter(|b| b["text"] != "[Local image reference; pixels not provided]")
            .collect();
        let supports = |b: &&Value| {
            b["page"].as_u64() == Some(citation.page as u64)
                && !citation.quote.trim().is_empty()
                && b["text"]
                    .as_str()
                    .is_some_and(|text| text.contains(&citation.quote))
        };
        let exact = blocks.iter().find(|b| b["id"] == citation.block_id);
        if let Some(b) = exact {
            valid &= supports(b);
        } else {
            let matches: Vec<_> = blocks
                .iter()
                .filter(|b| b["parent_block_id"] == citation.block_id && supports(b))
                .collect();
            if matches.len() == 1 {
                citation.block_id = matches[0]["id"].as_str().unwrap_or("").into();
            } else {
                valid = false;
            }
        }
    }
    valid
}

impl Chat {
    /// Clear exactly this process and full/chunk scope; other histories are unaffected.
    pub fn clear(&mut self, target: &str, chunk: Option<usize>) {
        self.histories.remove(&(target.into(), chunk));
    }

    /// Read selected HTML, cover all evidence in bounded batches, and return validated scoped answers.
    #[allow(clippy::too_many_arguments)]
    pub fn ask(
        &mut self,
        data: &Data,
        decisions: &[Decision],
        documents: &BTreeMap<String, String>,
        run: &Path,
        model: &dyn Model,
        target: &str,
        question: &str,
        chunk: Option<usize>,
    ) -> Result<Answer> {
        let file = documents
            .get(target)
            .context("Select a classified process document")?;
        let path = run
            .join(file)
            .canonicalize()
            .context("Cannot read process HTML; reconvert")?;
        ensure!(
            path.starts_with(run.canonicalize()?),
            "Process HTML path is outside this run."
        );
        let sections = selected(data, decisions, Some(target));
        let mut contexts = read_html(&path, &sections, &[])?;
        if let Some(number) = chunk {
            ensure!(
                number > 0 && number <= contexts.len(),
                "Chunk number is outside selected process."
            );
            contexts = vec![contexts[number - 1].clone()];
        }
        if contexts.is_empty() {
            return Ok(abstain("No evidence is assigned to this process."));
        }
        let key = (target.to_string(), chunk);
        let previous = self.histories.get(&key).cloned().unwrap_or_default();
        let mut history = previous.iter().rev().take(4).cloned().collect::<Vec<_>>();
        history.reverse();
        self.notice.clear();
        let scope = chunk
            .map(|n| format!("section chunk {n}"))
            .unwrap_or_else(|| "full process".into());
        let schema = answer_schema();
        let payload = |evidence: &[Value], history: &[Value], scope: &str| json!({"process":target,"scope":scope,"evidence":evidence,"history":history,"question":question});
        while !history.is_empty()
            && (serde_json::to_string(&history)?.chars().count() > model.budget() / 4
                || !fits(
                    model,
                    CHAT_INSTRUCTION,
                    &payload(&[], &history, &scope),
                    &schema,
                ))
        {
            history.remove(0);
        }
        if history.len() != previous.len() {
            self.notice="Only recent conversation turns fit; all selected evidence is still checked. Restate earlier details if needed.".into();
        }
        let mut batches: Vec<Vec<Value>> = vec![];
        let mut current: Vec<Value> = vec![];
        for section in contexts {
            for block in section["blocks"]
                .as_array()
                .context("Invalid internal evidence blocks")?
            {
                // Append in place, then undo just this block if the exact request budget
                // is exceeded. This avoids cloning the entire growing batch per text line.
                if current
                    .last()
                    .is_some_and(|s| s["section_id"] == section["section_id"])
                {
                    current.last_mut().expect("last section")["blocks"]
                        .as_array_mut()
                        .expect("blocks")
                        .push(block.clone());
                } else {
                    current.push(json!({"section_id":section["section_id"],"blocks":[block]}));
                }
                if !fits(
                    model,
                    CHAT_INSTRUCTION,
                    &payload(&current, &history, &scope),
                    &schema,
                ) {
                    let last_blocks = current.last_mut().expect("last section")["blocks"]
                        .as_array_mut()
                        .expect("blocks");
                    let overflow = last_blocks.pop().expect("appended block");
                    if last_blocks.is_empty() {
                        current.pop();
                    }
                    if !current.is_empty() {
                        batches.push(std::mem::take(&mut current));
                    }
                    current.push(json!({"section_id":section["section_id"],"blocks":[overflow]}));
                    ensure!(
                        fits(
                            model,
                            CHAT_INSTRUCTION,
                            &payload(&current, &history, &scope),
                            &schema
                        ),
                        "One evidence line, question or history exceeds context budget. Use /clear, shorten question or increase --context-chars."
                    );
                }
            }
        }
        if !current.is_empty() {
            batches.push(current);
        }
        if batches.len() > 1 {
            self.notice.push_str(&format!(" Checked all selected evidence in {} batches; answers below are scoped to each batch.",batches.len()));
        }
        let mut answers = vec![];
        let mut citations = vec![];
        let mut invalid = false;
        for (index, batch) in batches.iter().enumerate() {
            eprintln!("Checking evidence batch {}/{}…", index + 1, batches.len());
            let batch_scope = if batches.len() == 1 {
                scope.clone()
            } else {
                format!("{scope}; evidence batch {}/{}", index + 1, batches.len())
            };
            let mut request = payload(batch, &history, &batch_scope);
            let mut accepted = None;
            for _ in 0..2 {
                let value = model.complete(CHAT_INSTRUCTION, &request, &schema)?;
                ensure!(validate(&value, &schema), "Invalid answer result schema.");
                let mut answer: Answer = serde_json::from_value(value)?;
                if valid_citations(&mut answer, batch) {
                    accepted = Some(answer);
                    break;
                }
                request["citation_feedback"] = json!(
                    "Citations failed validation. Use a supplied text id, its section_id and page, and an exact quote from that one line. Otherwise abstain."
                );
            }
            match accepted {
                None => invalid = true,
                Some(answer) if !answer.abstain => {
                    answers.push(if batches.len() == 1 {
                        answer.answer
                    } else {
                        format!(
                            "Evidence batch {}/{}: {}",
                            index + 1,
                            batches.len(),
                            answer.answer
                        )
                    });
                    for c in answer.citations {
                        if !citations.contains(&c) {
                            citations.push(c);
                        }
                    }
                }
                _ => {}
            }
        }
        if invalid {
            return Ok(abstain(
                "Abstained: the model supplied unsupported or foreign citations after one correction.",
            ));
        }
        let result = if answers.is_empty() {
            abstain(
                "Abstained: the model could not support an answer from selected HTML text. Use /evidence to inspect it and /images for screenshots the model cannot read.",
            )
        } else {
            Answer {
                answer: answers.join("\n\n"),
                abstain: false,
                citations,
            }
        };
        history.push(json!({"question":question,"response":result}));
        if history.len() > 4 {
            history.remove(0);
            self.notice.push_str(" Conversation memory now retains the latest four turns; source evidence is unchanged.");
        }
        self.histories.insert(key, history);
        Ok(result)
    }
}

//! Offline regressions: real PDFs/Poppler/HTML, injected model results, and loopback HTTP.
//! No external model, API key or user document is needed by this suite.
use super::*;
use crate::client::{Model, answer_schema, decision_schema, validate};
use serde_json::{Value, json};
use std::{
    cell::RefCell,
    collections::HashSet,
    io::{Read, Write},
    net::TcpListener,
    thread,
};

/// Write a minimal standards-compliant PDF with text, images and known source grouping.
fn fixture(path: &Path, pages: &[Vec<&str>]) {
    let mut objects=vec![b"<< /Type /Catalog /Pages 2 0 R >>".to_vec(),vec![],
        b"<< /Type /Font /Subtype /Type1 /BaseFont /Helvetica >>".to_vec(),
        b"<< /Type /XObject /Subtype /Image /Width 1 /Height 1 /ColorSpace /DeviceRGB /BitsPerComponent 8 /Filter /ASCIIHexDecode /Length 7 >>\nstream\n336699>\nendstream".to_vec()];
    let mut kids = vec![];
    for lines in pages {
        let id = objects.len() + 1;
        kids.push(format!("{id} 0 R"));
        objects.push(format!("<< /Type /Page /Parent 2 0 R /MediaBox [0 0 600 800] /Resources << /Font << /F1 3 0 R >> /XObject << /Im1 4 0 R >> >> /Contents {} 0 R >>",id+1).into_bytes());
        let mut stream = String::new();
        for (i, line) in lines.iter().enumerate() {
            let line = line
                .replace('\\', "\\\\")
                .replace('(', "\\(")
                .replace(')', "\\)");
            stream.push_str(&format!(
                "BT /F1 11 Tf 40 {} Td ({line}) Tj ET\n",
                750 - i * 22
            ));
        }
        // A real raster reference follows the text; plain-page fallback keeps it attached.
        stream.push_str("q 40 0 0 40 40 100 cm /Im1 Do Q\n");
        objects.push(
            format!(
                "<< /Length {} >>\nstream\n{}endstream",
                stream.len(),
                stream
            )
            .into_bytes(),
        );
    }
    objects[1] = format!(
        "<< /Type /Pages /Count {} /Kids [{}] >>",
        pages.len(),
        kids.join(" ")
    )
    .into_bytes();
    let mut pdf = b"%PDF-1.4\n".to_vec();
    let mut offsets = vec![0];
    for (i, obj) in objects.iter().enumerate() {
        offsets.push(pdf.len());
        pdf.extend_from_slice(format!("{} 0 obj\n", i + 1).as_bytes());
        pdf.extend_from_slice(obj);
        pdf.extend_from_slice(b"\nendobj\n");
    }
    let xref = pdf.len();
    pdf.extend_from_slice(
        format!("xref\n0 {}\n0000000000 65535 f \n", objects.len() + 1).as_bytes(),
    );
    for offset in &offsets[1..] {
        pdf.extend_from_slice(format!("{offset:010} 00000 n \n").as_bytes());
    }
    pdf.extend_from_slice(
        format!(
            "trailer\n<< /Size {} /Root 1 0 R >>\nstartxref\n{xref}\n%%EOF\n",
            objects.len() + 1
        )
        .as_bytes(),
    );
    fs::write(path, pdf).unwrap();
}
/// Disposable real conversion with arbitrary similar names and literal HTML-special characters.
fn setup() -> (tempfile::TempDir, PathBuf, Data) {
    let root = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let pdf = root.path().join("source.pdf");
    let run = root.path().join("run");
    fixture(
        &pdf,
        &[
            vec![
                "Process: invoice-flow",
                "Operation: stop",
                "Result: inactive & verified <ok>",
            ],
            vec![
                "Process: invoice-flow-extended",
                "Operation: start",
                "Result: active",
            ],
            vec![
                "Process: invoice-flow",
                "Operation: health review",
                "Result: stable",
            ],
        ],
    );
    let data = extract(&pdf, &run, 4).unwrap();
    (root, run, data)
}
/// Deterministic model oracle, checking text-only requests and retaining payloads for assertions.
struct Oracle {
    budget: usize,
    calls: RefCell<Vec<Value>>,
    answer: RefCell<Vec<Value>>,
}
impl Oracle {
    /// Default available model with an optional queued invalid response for repair tests.
    fn new(budget: usize) -> Self {
        Self {
            budget,
            calls: RefCell::new(vec![]),
            answer: RefCell::new(vec![]),
        }
    }
}
impl Model for Oracle {
    fn budget(&self) -> usize {
        self.budget
    }
    fn complete(&self, instruction: &str, payload: &Value, schema: &Value) -> Result<Value> {
        let serialized = payload.to_string();
        assert!(!serialized.contains("data:image"));
        assert!(!serialized.contains("images/"));
        assert!(!serialized.contains("image_url"));
        assert!(
            crate::client::messages(instruction, payload, schema)
                .to_string()
                .chars()
                .count()
                <= self.budget
        );
        self.calls.borrow_mut().push(payload.clone());
        if schema == &decision_schema() {
            let section = &payload["evidence"];
            let name = section["blocks"]
                .as_array()
                .unwrap()
                .iter()
                .filter_map(|b| b["text"].as_str())
                .find_map(|s| s.strip_prefix("Process: "))
                .unwrap();
            return Ok(
                json!({"section_id":section["section_id"],"status":"assigned","target":name,"confidence":0.95,"reason":"Controlled test oracle, not measured accuracy"}),
            );
        }
        if !self.answer.borrow().is_empty() {
            return Ok(self.answer.borrow_mut().remove(0));
        }
        for section in payload["evidence"].as_array().unwrap() {
            for b in section["blocks"].as_array().unwrap() {
                if b["text"] != "[Local image reference; pixels not provided]"
                    && !b["text"].as_str().unwrap().trim().is_empty()
                {
                    return Ok(
                        json!({"answer":b["text"],"abstain":false,"citations":[{"section_id":section["section_id"],"block_id":b["id"],"page":b["page"],"quote":b["text"]}]}),
                    );
                }
            }
        }
        Ok(json!({"answer":"","abstain":true,"citations":[]}))
    }
}

#[test]
fn real_pdf_grouping_images_html_and_conservation() {
    let (_root, run, data) = setup();
    let model = Oracle::new(24000);
    assert_eq!(data.pages.len(), 3);
    assert_eq!(data.sections.len(), 3);
    let source_ids: HashSet<_> = data
        .pages
        .iter()
        .flat_map(|p| &p.blocks)
        .map(|b| &b.id)
        .collect();
    let owners: Vec<_> = data
        .sections
        .iter()
        .flat_map(|s| &s.blocks)
        .chain(&data.furniture)
        .map(|b| &b.id)
        .collect();
    assert_eq!(owners.len(), source_ids.len());
    assert_eq!(owners.into_iter().collect::<HashSet<_>>(), source_ids);
    let decisions = classify(&data, None, &model, &run).unwrap();
    let groups = discovered(&decisions);
    assert_eq!(groups, vec!["invoice-flow", "invoice-flow-extended"]);
    let docs = export(&data, &groups, &decisions, &run, "complete").unwrap();
    let sections = selected(&data, &decisions, Some("invoice-flow"));
    assert_eq!(sections.len(), 2);
    let text = fs::read_to_string(run.join(&docs["invoice-flow"])).unwrap();
    assert!(!text.contains("invoice-flow-extended"));
    assert!(text.contains("&amp; verified &lt;ok&gt;"));
    assert!(text.contains("data:image/png;base64,"));
    assert_eq!(
        read_html(&run.join(&docs["invoice-flow"]), &sections, &[])
            .unwrap()
            .len(),
        2
    );
    let image = sections[0]
        .blocks
        .iter()
        .find(|b| b.kind == "image")
        .unwrap();
    assert!(
        image_for_process(&data, &decisions, Some("invoice-flow"), &image.id, &run)
            .unwrap()
            .is_file()
    );
    assert!(
        image_for_process(
            &data,
            &decisions,
            Some("invoice-flow-extended"),
            &image.id,
            &run
        )
        .is_err()
    );
}

#[test]
fn chat_scopes_history_citations_and_multipart_prompt() {
    let (_root, run, data) = setup();
    let model = Oracle::new(24000);
    let decisions = classify(&data, None, &model, &run).unwrap();
    let docs = export(&data, &discovered(&decisions), &decisions, &run, "complete").unwrap();
    model.calls.borrow_mut().clear();
    let mut chat = Chat::default();
    let result = chat
        .ask(
            &data,
            &decisions,
            &docs,
            &run,
            &model,
            "invoice-flow",
            "Has it started and stopped?",
            None,
        )
        .unwrap();
    assert!(!result.abstain);
    assert!(
        !model
            .calls
            .borrow()
            .last()
            .unwrap()
            .to_string()
            .contains("invoice-flow-extended")
    );
    assert!(CHAT_INSTRUCTION.contains("answer each supported part"));
    chat.ask(
        &data,
        &decisions,
        &docs,
        &run,
        &model,
        "invoice-flow-extended",
        "State?",
        None,
    )
    .unwrap();
    assert!(
        !model
            .calls
            .borrow()
            .last()
            .unwrap()
            .to_string()
            .contains("Has it started")
    );
    assert_eq!(chat.histories.len(), 2);
    chat.ask(
        &data,
        &decisions,
        &docs,
        &run,
        &model,
        "invoice-flow",
        "State?",
        Some(1),
    )
    .unwrap();
    assert_eq!(
        model.calls.borrow().last().unwrap()["scope"],
        "section chunk 1"
    );
    assert!(
        chat.ask(
            &data,
            &decisions,
            &docs,
            &run,
            &model,
            "invoice-flow",
            "State?",
            Some(99)
        )
        .is_err()
    );
    chat.clear("invoice-flow", None);
    assert!(!chat.histories.contains_key(&("invoice-flow".into(), None)));
}

#[test]
fn batching_visits_every_unit_and_trims_history() {
    let (_root, run, data) = setup();
    let large = Oracle::new(24000);
    let decisions = classify(&data, None, &large, &run).unwrap();
    let docs = export(&data, &discovered(&decisions), &decisions, &run, "complete").unwrap();
    let model = Oracle::new(3100);
    let mut chat = Chat::default();
    chat.histories.insert(
        ("invoice-flow".into(), None),
        vec![json!({"question":"old","response":"x".repeat(20000)})],
    );
    chat.ask(
        &data,
        &decisions,
        &docs,
        &run,
        &model,
        "invoice-flow",
        "What is recorded?",
        None,
    )
    .unwrap();
    let calls = model.calls.borrow();
    assert!(calls.len() > 1);
    assert!(chat.notice.contains("recent"));
    assert!(chat.notice.contains("batches"));
    let seen: Vec<_> = calls
        .iter()
        .flat_map(|p| p["evidence"].as_array().unwrap())
        .flat_map(|s| s["blocks"].as_array().unwrap())
        .map(|b| b["id"].as_str().unwrap().to_owned())
        .collect();
    let expected: Vec<_> = selected(&data, &decisions, Some("invoice-flow"))
        .iter()
        .flat_map(|s| &s.blocks)
        .map(|b| b.id.clone())
        .collect();
    assert_eq!(seen, expected);
}

#[test]
fn citation_repair_rejects_foreign_quotes_and_abstention_prose() {
    let (_root, run, data) = setup();
    let model = Oracle::new(24000);
    let decisions = classify(&data, None, &model, &run).unwrap();
    let docs = export(&data, &discovered(&decisions), &decisions, &run, "complete").unwrap();
    let bad = json!({"answer":"invented","abstain":false,"citations":[{"section_id":"foreign","block_id":"foreign","page":99,"quote":"invented"}]});
    model.answer.borrow_mut().extend([bad.clone(), bad]);
    let mut chat = Chat::default();
    assert!(
        chat.ask(
            &data,
            &decisions,
            &docs,
            &run,
            &model,
            "invoice-flow",
            "State?",
            None
        )
        .unwrap()
        .abstain
    );
    assert!(chat.histories.is_empty());
    model
        .answer
        .borrow_mut()
        .push(json!({"answer":"invented","abstain":true,"citations":[]}));
    let answer = chat
        .ask(
            &data,
            &decisions,
            &docs,
            &run,
            &model,
            "invoice-flow",
            "State?",
            None,
        )
        .unwrap();
    assert!(!answer.answer.contains("invented"));
    model
        .answer
        .borrow_mut()
        .push(json!({"answer":"invalid citation","abstain":false,"citations":[]}));
    assert!(
        !chat
            .ask(
                &data,
                &decisions,
                &docs,
                &run,
                &model,
                "invoice-flow",
                "State?",
                None
            )
            .unwrap()
            .abstain
    );
}

#[test]
fn tampered_missing_and_swapped_html_fail_before_calls() {
    let (_root, run, data) = setup();
    let model = Oracle::new(24000);
    let decisions = classify(&data, None, &model, &run).unwrap();
    let mut docs = export(&data, &discovered(&decisions), &decisions, &run, "complete").unwrap();
    let mut chat = Chat::default();
    model.calls.borrow_mut().clear();
    let path = run.join(&docs["invoice-flow"]);
    let original = fs::read_to_string(&path).unwrap();
    fs::write(
        &path,
        original.replace("Operation: stop", "Operation: FAKE"),
    )
    .unwrap();
    assert!(
        chat.ask(
            &data,
            &decisions,
            &docs,
            &run,
            &model,
            "invoice-flow",
            "State?",
            None
        )
        .is_err()
    );
    fs::remove_file(&path).unwrap();
    assert!(
        chat.ask(
            &data,
            &decisions,
            &docs,
            &run,
            &model,
            "invoice-flow",
            "State?",
            None
        )
        .is_err()
    );
    docs.insert("invoice-flow".into(), docs["invoice-flow-extended"].clone());
    assert!(
        chat.ask(
            &data,
            &decisions,
            &docs,
            &run,
            &model,
            "invoice-flow",
            "State?",
            None
        )
        .is_err()
    );
    assert!(model.calls.borrow().is_empty());
}

#[test]
fn slice_offset_and_invalid_page_count() {
    let root = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let path = root.path().join("Any_pages_11-12.pdf");
    fixture(&path, &[vec!["Process: x"], vec!["Process: y"]]);
    let data = extract(&path, &root.path().join("good"), 4).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(data.pages[0].number, 11);
    assert_eq!(data.pages[1].number, 12);
    let wrong = root.path().join("Any_pages_11-13.pdf");
    fs::copy(&path, &wrong).unwrap();
    assert!(extract(&wrong, &root.path().join("bad"), 4).is_err());
}

#[test]
fn explicit_boundaries_cross_pages_and_furniture_conserve_rows() {
    let (_root, _run, mut data) = setup();
    let mut page1 = data.pages[0].clone();
    let mut page2 = data.pages[1].clone();
    page1.blocks.retain(|b| b.kind == "text");
    page2.blocks.retain(|b| b.kind == "text");
    page1.blocks[0].text = "Evidence: arbitrary-process".into();
    page1.blocks[1].text = "Repeated table row: retain".into();
    page1.blocks[2].text = "Operational Evidence Record".into();
    page2.blocks[0].text = "Repeated table row: retain".into();
    page2.blocks[1].text = "Evidence section closed".into();
    page2.blocks[2].text = "orphan".into();
    data.pages = vec![page1, page2];
    let (sections, furniture) = segment(&data.pages).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(furniture.len(), 1);
    assert_eq!(sections.len(), 2);
    assert_eq!(sections[0].boundary, None);
    assert!(sections[1].boundary.is_some());
    assert_eq!(
        sections[0]
            .blocks
            .iter()
            .filter(|b| b.text == "Repeated table row: retain")
            .count(),
        2
    );
    assert!(sections[0].blocks.iter().any(|b| b.page == 2));
}

#[test]
fn export_refuses_missing_duplicate_and_inconsistent_decisions() {
    let (_root, run, data) = setup();
    let mut decisions = pending(&data);
    assert!(export(&data, &[], &decisions[..1], &run, "complete").is_err());
    decisions[1] = decisions[0].clone();
    assert!(export(&data, &[], &decisions, &run, "complete").is_err());
    decisions = pending(&data);
    decisions[0].target = Some("wrong".into());
    assert!(export(&data, &[], &decisions, &run, "complete").is_err());
}

#[test]
fn schema_rejects_extra_fields_wrong_types_and_invalid_confidence() {
    let mut value = json!({"answer":"ok","abstain":true,"citations":[]});
    assert!(validate(&value, &answer_schema()));
    value["extra"] = json!(1);
    assert!(!validate(&value, &answer_schema()));
    let mut d =
        json!({"section_id":"s","status":"assigned","target":"a","confidence":1.1,"reason":"x"});
    assert!(!validate(&d, &decision_schema()));
    d["confidence"] = json!(0.9);
    assert!(validate(&d, &decision_schema()));
    d["status"] = json!("invented");
    assert!(!validate(&d, &decision_schema()));
}

#[test]
fn paths_and_terminal_protocols_are_local() {
    let root = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let path = root.path().join("my document.pdf");
    fixture(&path, &[vec!["Process: sample"]]);
    for value in [
        path.display().to_string(),
        format!("'{}'", path.display()),
        path.display().to_string().replace(' ', "\\ "),
    ] {
        assert_eq!(pdf_path(&value).unwrap(), path.canonicalize().unwrap());
    }
    assert!(pdf_path(&root.path().display().to_string()).is_err());
    assert!(image_escape(b"PNG", "kitty").contains("a=T,f=100,q=2,m=0;UE5H"));
    assert!(image_escape(b"PNG", "iterm2").contains("size=3;inline=1"));
    assert!(!safe("danger\x1b[31m").contains('\x1b'));
}

/// One-shot local HTTP endpoint capturing an actual reqwest JSON body/auth header.
fn server(response: Value, status: u16) -> (String, thread::JoinHandle<String>) {
    let listener = TcpListener::bind("127.0.0.1:0").unwrap();
    let url = format!(
        "http://{}/custom/v1?api-version=test",
        listener.local_addr().unwrap()
    );
    let handle = thread::spawn(move || {
        let (mut socket, _) = listener.accept().unwrap();
        socket
            .set_read_timeout(Some(std::time::Duration::from_secs(5)))
            .unwrap();
        let mut bytes = vec![];
        let mut buffer = [0; 4096];
        loop {
            let count = socket.read(&mut buffer).unwrap();
            if count == 0 {
                break;
            }
            bytes.extend_from_slice(&buffer[..count]);
            if let Some(end) = bytes.windows(4).position(|w| w == b"\r\n\r\n") {
                let headers = String::from_utf8_lossy(&bytes[..end]).to_lowercase();
                let size = headers
                    .lines()
                    .find_map(|s| s.strip_prefix("content-length: "))
                    .unwrap_or("0")
                    .parse::<usize>()
                    .unwrap();
                if bytes.len() >= end + 4 + size {
                    break;
                }
            }
        }
        let body = response.to_string();
        write!(socket,"HTTP/1.1 {status} Test\r\nContent-Type: application/json\r\nContent-Length: {}\r\nConnection: close\r\n\r\n{body}",body.len()).unwrap();
        String::from_utf8(bytes).unwrap()
    });
    (url, handle)
}

#[test]
fn actual_http_url_auth_schema_and_secret_safe_failures() {
    let answer = json!({"answer":"insufficient","abstain":true,"citations":[]});
    let envelope =
        json!({"choices":[{"finish_reason":"stop","message":{"content":answer.to_string()}}]});
    let (url, handle) = server(envelope, 200);
    let client = Client::new(
        &url,
        "test-key".into(),
        "mock".into(),
        "json_schema",
        "api-key",
        "base",
        24000,
        true,
    )
    .unwrap();
    assert_eq!(
        client
            .complete("Test", &json!({}), &answer_schema())
            .unwrap(),
        answer
    );
    let request = handle.join().unwrap();
    assert!(request.starts_with("POST /custom/v1/chat/completions?api-version=test "));
    assert!(request.to_lowercase().contains("api-key: test-key"));
    assert!(request.contains("json_schema"));
    for code in [401, 403, 404, 429, 500, 302] {
        let (url, handle) = server(json!({"secret":"test-key"}), code);
        let client = Client::new(
            &url,
            "test-key".into(),
            "mock".into(),
            "json_object",
            "bearer",
            "base",
            24000,
            true,
        )
        .unwrap();
        let error = client
            .complete("Test", &json!({}), &answer_schema())
            .unwrap_err()
            .to_string();
        assert!(!error.contains("test-key"));
        handle.join().unwrap();
    }
}

#[test]
fn endpoint_consent_and_context_fail_before_network() {
    for endpoint in [
        "http://remote.example/v1",
        "https://u:p@example.com",
        "https://example.com?api_key=secret",
        "https://example.com/#secret",
        "https://example.com:999999",
    ] {
        assert!(
            Client::new(
                endpoint,
                "secret".into(),
                "mock".into(),
                "json_object",
                "bearer",
                "auto",
                24000,
                true
            )
            .is_err()
        );
    }
    let client = Client::new(
        "https://example.invalid/v1",
        "secret".into(),
        "mock".into(),
        "json_object",
        "bearer",
        "auto",
        24000,
        false,
    )
    .unwrap();
    assert!(
        client
            .complete("Test", &json!({}), &answer_schema())
            .unwrap_err()
            .to_string()
            .contains("consent")
    );
    let small = Client::new(
        "https://example.invalid/v1",
        "secret".into(),
        "mock".into(),
        "json_object",
        "bearer",
        "auto",
        50,
        true,
    )
    .unwrap();
    assert!(
        small
            .complete("Test", &json!({}), &answer_schema())
            .unwrap_err()
            .to_string()
            .contains("budget")
    );
}

/// Inject a routing result to exercise semantic checks after schema validation.
struct Fixed(Value);
impl Model for Fixed {
    fn budget(&self) -> usize {
        24000
    }
    fn complete(&self, _: &str, _: &Value, _: &Value) -> Result<Value> {
        Ok(self.0.clone())
    }
}

#[test]
fn discovered_labels_must_be_exact_and_decisions_must_match_sections() {
    let (_root, run, data) = setup();
    let valid = json!({"section_id":data.sections[0].id,"status":"assigned","target":"invoice-flow","confidence":0.9,"reason":"test"});
    for (field, value) in [
        ("target", json!("invoice")),
        ("target", json!("invented")),
        ("section_id", json!("foreign")),
        ("status", json!("mixed")),
        ("target", Value::Null),
    ] {
        let mut bad = valid.clone();
        bad[field] = value;
        assert!(classify(&data, None, &Fixed(bad), &run).is_err());
    }
}

#[test]
fn partial_answer_keeps_verified_stop_without_inventing_start() {
    let (_root, run, data) = setup();
    let model = Oracle::new(24000);
    let decisions = classify(&data, None, &model, &run).unwrap();
    let docs = export(&data, &discovered(&decisions), &decisions, &run, "complete").unwrap();
    let section = &selected(&data, &decisions, Some("invoice-flow"))[0];
    let line = section
        .blocks
        .iter()
        .find(|b| b.text == "Operation: stop")
        .unwrap();
    model.answer.borrow_mut().push(json!({"answer":"A stop is documented. A start is not established by the supplied text.","abstain":false,
        "citations":[{"section_id":section.id,"block_id":line.id,"page":line.page,"quote":line.text}]}));
    let answer = Chat::default()
        .ask(
            &data,
            &decisions,
            &docs,
            &run,
            &model,
            "invoice-flow",
            "Has it started and stopped?",
            None,
        )
        .unwrap();
    assert!(!answer.abstain);
    assert!(answer.answer.contains("not established"));
    assert_eq!(answer.citations[0].quote, "Operation: stop");
}

#[cfg(unix)]
#[test]
fn image_symlink_cannot_escape_run() {
    let (root, run, data) = setup();
    let model = Oracle::new(24000);
    let decisions = classify(&data, None, &model, &run).unwrap();
    let b = data.sections[0]
        .blocks
        .iter()
        .find(|b| b.kind == "image")
        .unwrap();
    let path = run.join(b.path.as_ref().unwrap());
    let outside = root.path().join("outside.png");
    fs::write(&outside, b"not evidence").unwrap();
    fs::remove_file(&path).unwrap();
    std::os::unix::fs::symlink(outside, path).unwrap();
    assert!(image_for_process(&data, &decisions, Some("invoice-flow"), &b.id, &run).is_err());
}

#[test]
fn parallel_conversion_matches_serial_and_keeps_cross_worker_sections() {
    let root = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let pdf = root.path().join("cross-boundary.pdf");
    fixture(
        &pdf,
        &[
            vec!["Evidence: billing-task", "Operation: start"],
            vec!["Operation: stop", "Evidence section closed"],
            vec![
                "Evidence: shipping-task",
                "Operation: start",
                "Evidence section closed",
            ],
        ],
    );
    let serial = extract(&pdf, &root.path().join("serial"), 1).unwrap();
    let parallel = extract(&pdf, &root.path().join("parallel"), 4).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(
        serde_json::to_value(&serial).unwrap(),
        serde_json::to_value(&parallel).unwrap()
    );
    assert_eq!(parallel.sections[0].boundary, None);
    assert_eq!(
        parallel.sections[0]
            .blocks
            .iter()
            .map(|b| b.page)
            .collect::<HashSet<_>>(),
        HashSet::from([1, 2])
    );
}
Source snapshots are included for exact reconstruction. They are not a claim of live-provider accuracy. Maintain the original evidence, process isolation and quote checks when extending this application.
